- **qralph**: Deterministic multi-agent pipeline that takes ideas from concept to deployed production code. Handles the full lifecycle — ideation, persona generation, concept review, planning, parallel execution, quality loops, verification, demo, deployment, and smoke testing. Use this whenever the user says /qralph, QRALPH, asks to "run the pipeline", or wants end-to-end multi-agent project execution. Also use when the user references pipeline phases, project orchestration, or wants to build something from scratch with automated quality gates. ([sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills](https://github.com/sparkst/sparkry-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/qralph/skills/qralph))
- **qraspi-architecture**: QRASPI Architecture phase — locks the path-dependent decisions for a NEW system as MADR ADRs (with alternatives), draws the C4 Context + Container in Mermaid, and specifies required fitness functions. Use for '/qraspi-architecture &lt;project&gt;', 'write the ADRs for new X', 'what architecture for new X', 'lock the stack decisions for X'. Not for critiquing an EXISTING system (architecture-review), retrospective ADR journaling (architecture-journal), a feature in an existing codebase (qrspi-spec), or the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-architecture) · ★3)
- **qraspi-graduate**: QRASPI Graduation — the terminal handoff from greenfield (QRASPI) to brownfield (QRSPI). Once the walking skeleton is green and V0/V1 ships, captures repo + accepted ADRs + skeleton state + fitness functions + stack into graduation.md and hands new feature work to QRSPI. Use for '/qraspi-graduate &lt;project&gt;', 'graduate this to QRSPI', 'V1 is done, hand off to the feature workflow'. Do not fire mid-workflow or on a generic 'we are done'/'ship it' — this is the END of QRASPI. Not for the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-graduate) · ★3)
- **qraspi-implement**: QRASPI Implement phase — grows the green walking skeleton ONE approved slice at a time with strict Red-Green-Refactor per phase, keeping fitness gates green, and writes a per-slice proof log. Use for '/qraspi-implement &lt;project&gt;', 'implement the slice on the skeleton', 'build the approved plan-{slice} with RGR'. Requires plan-{slice}.md status: approved and a green skeleton. Not for a feature in an EXISTING codebase (qrspi-implement), the deprecated RPI workflow, or a bare TDD cycle with no plan (tdd-loop). ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-implement) · ★3)
- **qraspi-plan**: QRASPI Plan phase — converts the next slice from the skeleton backlog into a mechanically executable, vertically-sliced plan-{slice}.md with exact file paths and per-phase verification, grown on the green walking skeleton. Use for '/qraspi-plan &lt;project&gt;', 'plan the first slice of new X', 'plan the next increment on the skeleton'. Consumes skeleton.md and REFUSES horizontal-layer plans. Not for a feature in an EXISTING codebase (qrspi-plan) or the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-plan) · ★3)
- **qraspi-questions**: QRASPI Questions phase -- surface what is unknown about a NEW system before any research, architecture, or skeleton begins. Use for "/qraspi-questions &lt;project&gt;", "new project from scratch", "greenfield X", "questions for a brand-new system". Do NOT use for QRSPI (an EXISTING codebase / adding a feature) -- that routes to qrspi-questions. Do NOT use for the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-questions) · ★3)
- **qraspi-research**: QRASPI Research phase -- map the solution LANDSCAPE for a new system, factual only, no recommendations. Use for "/qraspi-research &lt;project&gt;", "research the solution landscape for new X", "what libraries / prior art exist for X", "survey the options before architecting X". Mode switch: external-domain (no codebase) or inherited-repo. Do NOT use for QRSPI (an EXISTING codebase / adding a feature) -- that routes to qrspi-research. Do NOT use for the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-research) · ★3)
- **qraspi-skeleton**: QRASPI Skeleton phase — stands up a RUNNABLE walking skeleton for a NEW system: scaffolds the repo from accepted ADRs, walks one vertical slice end-to-end through every layer, lands the specified fitness functions as CI gates, and proves it with a real CI run (exit 0). Use for '/qraspi-skeleton &lt;project&gt;', 'scaffold the walking skeleton for X', 'stand up V0 of X with CI'. Exit gate is CI green, not a claim. Not for a single feature slice in an existing repo (*-feature-slice scaffolders), QRSPI (existing codebase), or the deprecated RPI workflow. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/qraspi-skeleton) · ★3)

- **qu-ai-wei**: 中文深度改写去 AI 味。对已有简体中文做保真深改或整篇重组，保留全部实质信息、作者语气和证据边界。 ([0mlkrizzz655335v/claude-skills-zh](https://github.com/0mlkrizzz655335v/claude-skills-zh/tree/HEAD/codex-skills-pack/skills/qu-ai-wei) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **qu-ai-wei**: 中文稿去 AI 味、去模板腔的改写规则库，合并三套规则（写作经验 + 信息守恒 + 语料实测），并逐条裁决它们互相矛盾的七处。MUST USE 当用户说「去AI味」「去味」「这段像 AI 写的」「改得像人写的」「降降机器味」「看看有没有 AI 腔」，或中文稿（公众号长文 / 小红书笔记 / 群公告 / 视频口播稿）定稿前要去掉翻案腔、顿号罗列、相邻句同款、破折号滥用、序数词小标题、拟人化喻体、起手式、段首零主语评论这些痕迹时。分「自家稿」与「白名单」两种模式，先过信息守恒、最小改动、结构不动三条硬边界，再动刀。NOT for：病句与语法（用 chinese-grammar-proofreader）、排版格式（chinese-format-lint）、英文稿（humanizer）、AI 味检测脚本与分级报告（lieflat-less-ai-tone）。 ([xhqing/social-agent](https://github.com/xhqing/social-agent/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/qu-ai-wei) · ★3)

- **qu-ai-wei**: 去AI味——修改由 AI 生成或 AI 辅助写成的中文文稿，让它读起来像一个有材料、有判断的人写的。当用户说"去AI味"、"AI味太重"、"太像AI写的"、"有机器味"、"读着很空/很套话"、"帮我改得像人写的"、"润色这篇AI写的稿子"、"降AI痕迹"时使用本 skill；用户贴出一段明显是 AI 腔的文字并要求修改、润色、重写时，即使没有说"AI味"三个字也应使用。适用于论文、报告、公众号文章、商品文案、汇报材料等各类中文文稿，也可用于自己起草长文后的自查。本 skill 的目标是改善文章本身，不是针对某个 AIGC 检测器降低百分比。 ([kayz/skills](https://github.com/kayz/skills/tree/HEAD/qu-ai-wei))

It measures how effectively uncertainty thresholds filter out incorrect predictions while preserving correct ones, rewarding high confidence in accurate regions and penalizing the loss of correct predictions when filtering uncertain voxels. - **qu-gongwen-aiwei**: 公文与半学术中文的写作、改稿与核查。用于通知、请示、报告、通报、函、纪要、意见、决定、批复、工作总结、汇报材料、讲话稿、制度办法、实施方案，以及课题报告、论文、开题与结项材料、评审意见、申报书这类以事实、依据、责任和文号为主体的文体。当用户要写、改、润色、压缩或核查这类材料，说「改成公文腔」「写得正式点」「像红头文件」「去公文 AI 味」「去掉公文里的 AI 味」「润色公文」「公文润色」「公文改稿」「压缩成汇报稿」「这段太 AI 但别改坏内容」时使用。也用于判断这类文本里哪些是 AI 套话、哪些是必须保留的公文规范用语。不用于小说、自媒体随笔、口语化评论。 ([Euterpe1145141919810/qu-gongwen-aiwei](https://github.com/Euterpe1145141919810/qu-gongwen-aiwei/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **qu-shi-ru-meng-shou**: 何时调用：当用户识别到稍纵即逝的机会但犹豫不决，或需要培养果断行动的能力时。 关键信号："机会来了但我还没准备好""我再想想""万一判断错了怎么办"。 何时不调用：用户需要的是谨慎分析（用 gui-jian-xun-huan 判断周期），或用户需要的是长期布局（用 chang-qi-zhu-yi）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/huo-zhi-lie-zhuan/qu-shi-ru-meng-shou) · ★1)
